===== M0001 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1232-1234
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1232 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1233 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1234 |   }

===== M0005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 122-172
Documentation:

Method code:
122 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
123 |       // null is the smallest value
124 |       if (p2 == null) {
125 |         if (p1 == null) {
126 |           return 0;
127 |         } else {
128 |           return P1_GT_P2;
129 |         }
130 |       }
131 | 
132 |       // check level
133 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
134 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
135 |       }
136 | 
137 |       // sourceName comparison
138 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
139 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
140 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
141 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
142 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
143 |           return sourceCompare;
144 |         }
145 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
146 |         return P1_LT_P2;
147 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
148 |         return P1_GT_P2;
149 |       }
150 |       // lineno comparison
151 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
152 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
153 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
154 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
155 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
156 |         return P1_LT_P2;
157 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
158 |         return P1_GT_P2;
159 |       }
160 |       // charno comparison
161 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
162 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
163 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
164 |         return charno1 - charno2;
165 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
166 |         return P1_LT_P2;
167 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
168 |         return P1_GT_P2;
169 |       }
170 |       // description
171 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
172 |     }

/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
457 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
458 |     {
459 |         final String defaultResource
460 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
461 | 
462 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
463 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
464 | 
465 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
466 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
467 | 
468 |         String formatString;
469 |         try {
470 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
471 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
472 |             throw new RuntimeException
473 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
474 |         }
475 |

477 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
478 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
479 |          * single 's.
480 |          */
481 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
482 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
483 |         return formatter.format(arguments);

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 59-81
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
59 |   @Override
60 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
61 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
62 |       return false;
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
66 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
67 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
68 |     if (pos >= 0) {
69 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
70 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 | 
75 |     if (!Character.isUpperCase(name.charAt(0))) {
76 |       return false;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // hack way of checking that there aren't any lower-case letters
80 |     return name.toUpperCase().equals(name);
81 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1074 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1075 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1076 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1077 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1078 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1079 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1080 |       } else {
1081 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1082 |       }
1083 |     }
1084 |     return builder.build();
1085 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
927 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
928 |                                       int startCharno,
929 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
930 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
931 |     Node typeNode = null;
932 | 
933 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
934 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
935 |     } else {
936 |       typeNode = parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
937 |     }
938 | 
939 |     if (typeNode != null && !matchingLC) {
940 |       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
941 |     }
942 | 
943 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
944 | 
945 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
946 |         matchingLC);
947 | 
948 |     return typeNode;
949 |   }

   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.

Method code:
69 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
70 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
71 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
72 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
73 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);

===== M0050 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 483-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
483 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
484 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
485 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
486 | 
487 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
488 | 
489 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
490 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
491 | 
492 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
493 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
494 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     curNode = n;
498 |     pushScope(n);
499 | 
500 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
501 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
502 |       // scope.
503 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
504 |     }
505 | 
506 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
507 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
508 | 
509 |     // Args
510 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
511 | 
512 |     // Body
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
514 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
515 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
516 | 
517 |     popScope();
518 |   }

55 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
56 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
57 |       addGuards(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards);
58 |     } else {
59 |       int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.guards, guard, guardComparator);
60 |       if (index < 0) {
61 |         index = -index - 1;
62 |       }
63 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
64 |     }
65 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 60-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
60 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
61 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
62 |     int c;
63 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
64 |     for (;;) {
65 |       // eat white spaces
66 |       for (;;) {
67 |         charno = -1;
68 |         c = getChar();
69 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
71 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
72 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
73 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
74 |           break;
75 |         }
76 |       }
77 | 
78 |       switch (c) {
79 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
80 |         case '@':
81 |           do {
82 |             c = getChar();
83 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
84 |               addToString(c);
85 |             } else {
86 |               ungetChar(c);
87 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
88 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
89 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
90 |             }
91 |           } while (true);
92 | 
93 |         case '*':
94 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
95 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
96 |           } else {
97 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
98 |           }
99 | 
100 |         case ',':
101 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
102 | 
103 |         case '>':
104 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
105 | 
106 |         case '(':
107 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
108 | 
109 |         case ')':
110 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
111 | 
112 |         case '{':
113 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
114 | 
115 |         case '}':
116 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
117 | 
118 |         case '[':
119 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
120 | 
121 |         case ']':
122 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
123 | 
124 |         case '?':
125 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
126 | 
127 |         case '!':
128 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
129 | 
130 |         case ':':
131 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
132 | 
133 |         case '=':
134 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
135 | 
136 |         case '|':
137 |           matchChar('|');
138 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
139 | 
140 |         case '.':
141 |           c = getChar();
142 |           if (c == '<') {
143 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
144 |           } else {
145 |             if (c == '.') {
146 |               c = getChar();
147 |               if (c == '.') {
148 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
149 |               } else {
150 |                 addToString('.');
151 |               }
152 |             }
153 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
154 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
155 |             c = '.';
156 |           }
157 |           // fall through
158 | 
159 |         default: {
160 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
161 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
162 |           int c1 = c;
163 |           addToString(c);
164 |           int c2 = getChar();
165 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
166 |             ungetChar(c2);
167 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
168 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
169 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
170 |           } else {
171 |             do {
172 |               c1 = c2;
173 |               c2 = getChar();
174 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
175 |                 ungetChar(c2);
176 |                 ungetChar(c1);
177 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
178 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
179 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
180 |               } else {
181 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                 } else {
184 |                   ungetChar(c2);
185 |                   addToString(c1);
186 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
187 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
188 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
189 |                 }
190 |               }
191 |             } while (true);
192 |           }
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 |     }
196 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CombinedCompilerPass.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CombinedCompilerPass.java
Original range: 141-152
Documentation:

Method code:
141 |   @Override
142 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     for (CallbackWrapper callback : callbacks) {
144 |       callback.shouldTraverseIfActive(t, n, parent);
145 |     }
146 |     // Note that this method could return false if all callbacks are inactive.
147 |     // This apparent optimization would make this method more expensive
148 |     // in the typical case where not all nodes are inactive. It is
149 |     // very unlikely that many all callbacks would be inactive at the same
150 |     // time (indeed, there are several checking passes that never return false).
151 |     return true;
152 |   }

   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens

704 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
705 |                         token = current();
706 | 
707 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
708 |                         ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
709 |                             extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
710 | 
711 |                         String returnDescription = returnDescriptionInfo.string;
712 | 
713 |                         if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
714 |                           jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
715 |                               returnDescription);
716 |                         }
717 | 
718 |                         token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
719 |                         continue retry;
720 | 
721 |                       case THIS:
722 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
723 |                           parser.addWarning(
724 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
725 |                         }
726 |                         break;
727 | 
728 |                       case TYPE:
729 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
730 |                           parser.addWarning(
731 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
732 |                         }
733 |                         break;
734 | 
735 |                       case TYPEDEF:
736 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
737 |                           parser.addWarning(
738 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
739 |                         }
740 |                         break;
741 |                     }
742 | 
743 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
744 |                   }
745 |                   continue retry;
746 |               }
747 |             }
748 |           }
749 |           break;
750 | 
751 |         case EOC:
752 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
753 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
754 |           }
755 | 
756 |           return true;
757 | 
758 |         case EOF:
759 |           // discard any accumulated information
760 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
761 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
762 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
763 |           return false;
764 | 
765 |         case EOL:
766 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
767 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
768 |           }
769 |           token = next();
770 |           continue retry;
771 | 
772 |         default:
773 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
774 |             token = next();
775 |             continue retry;
776 |           } else {
777 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
778 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
779 |             continue retry;
780 |           }
781 |       }
782 | 
783 |       // next token
784 |       token = next();
785 |     }
786 |   }

===== M0085 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckGlobalThis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckGlobalThis.java
Original range: 84-136
Documentation:
/**
   * Since this pass reports errors only when a global {@code this} keyword
   * is encountered, there is no reason to traverse non global contexts.
   */
Method code:
84 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
85 | 
86 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
87 |       // Don't traverse functions that are constructors or have the @this
88 |       // or @override annotation.
89 |       JSDocInfo jsDoc = getFunctionJsDocInfo(n);
90 |       if (jsDoc != null &&
91 |           (jsDoc.isConstructor() ||
92 |            jsDoc.hasThisType() ||
93 |            jsDoc.isOverride())) {
94 |         return false;
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Don't traverse functions unless they would normally
98 |       // be able to have a @this annotation associated with them. e.g.,
99 |       // var a = function() { }; // or
100 |       // function a() {} // or
101 |       // a.x = function() {};
102 |       int pType = parent.getType();
103 |       if (!(pType == Token.BLOCK ||
104 |             pType == Token.SCRIPT ||
105 |             pType == Token.NAME ||
106 |             pType == Token.ASSIGN)) {
107 |         return false;
108 |       }
109 |     }
110 | 
111 |     if (parent != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
112 |       Node lhs = parent.getFirstChild();
113 |       Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
114 |       
115 |       if (n == lhs) {
116 |         // Always traverse the left side of the assignment. To handle
117 |         // nested assignments properly (e.g., (a = this).property = c;),
118 |         // assignLhsChild should not be overridden.
119 |         if (assignLhsChild == null) {
120 |           assignLhsChild = lhs;
121 |         }
122 |       } else {
123 |         // Only traverse the right side if it's not an assignment to a prototype
124 |         // property or subproperty.
125 |           if (lhs.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
126 |               lhs.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
127 |             return false;
128 |           }
129 |           if (lhs.getQualifiedName() != null && lhs.getQualifiedName().contains(".prototype.")) {
130 |             return false;
131 |           }
132 |       }
133 |     }
134 | 
135 |     return true;
136 |   }

===== M0089 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 421-447
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
421 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
422 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
423 | 
424 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
425 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
426 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
427 |       return 0;
428 |     }
429 | 
430 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
431 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
432 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
433 |       // a new statistic may have been added
434 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
435 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
436 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
441 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
442 |       return 0;
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
446 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
447 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *

394 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
395 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
396 |       cursor++;
397 |       --ungetCursor;
398 |       if (charno == -1) {
399 |         charno = getOffset();
400 |       }
401 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
402 |     }
403 | 
404 |     for(;;) {
405 |       int c;
406 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
407 |         hitEOF = true;
408 |         if (charno == -1) {
409 |           charno = getOffset();
410 |         }
411 |         return EOF_CHAR;
412 |       }
413 |       cursor++;
414 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
415 | 
416 | 
417 |       if (c <= 127) {
418 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
419 |           lineEndChar = c;
420 |           c = '\n';
421 |         }
422 |       } else {
423 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
424 |           continue;
425 |         }
426 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
427 |           lineEndChar = c;
428 |           c = '\n';
429 |         }
430 |       }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 338-392
Documentation:

Method code:
338 |   protected int getChar() {
339 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
340 |       cursor++;
341 |       --ungetCursor;
342 |       if (charno == -1) {
343 |         charno = getOffset();
344 |       }
345 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     for(;;) {
349 |       int c;
350 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
351 |         hitEOF = true;
352 |         if (charno == -1) {
353 |           charno = getOffset();
354 |         }
355 |         return EOF_CHAR;
356 |       }
357 |       cursor++;
358 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
359 | 
360 | 
361 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
362 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
363 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
364 |           continue;
365 |         }
366 |         lineEndChar = -1;
367 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
368 |         lineno++;
369 |       }
370 | 
371 |       if (c <= 127) {
372 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
373 |           lineEndChar = c;
374 |           c = '\n';
375 |         }
376 |       } else {
377 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
378 |           continue;
379 |         }
380 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
381 |           lineEndChar = c;
382 |           c = '\n';
383 |         }
384 |       }
385 | 
386 |       if (charno == -1) {
387 |         charno = getOffset();
388 |       }
389 | 
390 |       return c;
391 |     }
392 |   }

===== M0157 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1435-1474
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1435 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1436 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1437 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1438 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1439 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1440 |       // {?} - right curly
1441 |       // {?=} - equals
1442 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1443 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1444 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1445 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1446 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1447 |       token = next();
1448 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1449 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1450 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1451 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1452 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1453 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1454 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1455 |       }
1456 | 
1457 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1458 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1459 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1460 |     } else {
1461 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1462 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1463 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1464 |           next();
1465 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1466 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1467 |           next();
1468 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1469 |         }
1470 |       }
1471 | 
1472 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1473 |     }
1474 |   }

===== M0173 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1480-1505
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1480 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1481 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1482 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1483 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1484 |       skipEOLs();
1485 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1486 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1487 |       skipEOLs();
1488 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1489 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1490 |       skipEOLs();
1491 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1492 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1493 |       String string = stream.getString();
1494 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1495 |         skipEOLs();
1496 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1497 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1498 |         return newStringNode(string);
1499 |       } else {
1500 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1501 |       }
1502 |     }
1503 | 
1504 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1505 |   }

===== M0174 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.fillEmptyModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 346-352
Documentation:
/**
   * Fill any empty modules with a place holder file. It makes any cross module
   * motion easier.
   */
Method code:

===== M0180 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 121-167
Documentation:

Method code:
121 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
122 |                                    String sourceString,
123 |                                    Config config,
124 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
125 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
126 |         config, errorReporter);
127 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
128 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
129 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
130 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
131 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
132 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
133 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
134 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
135 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
136 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
137 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
138 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
139 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC) {
140 |           JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser =
141 |               irFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(comment.getValue(),
142 |                   comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
143 |                   fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
144 |           if (jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo() != fileOverviewInfo) {
145 |             fileOverviewInfo = jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
146 |           } else {
147 |             JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
148 |             if (info != null) {
149 |               irFactory.attachJsDoc(comment, info);
150 |             }
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |       }
154 | 
155 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
156 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
157 |       // to that node.
158 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
159 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
160 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
161 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
162 |         }
163 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |     return irNode;
167 |   }

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RhinoErrorReporter.replacePlaceHolders(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RhinoErrorReporter.java
Original range: 65-68
Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private String replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
66 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
67 |     return s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q");
68 |   }

===== M0182 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 587-594
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, ObjectType owner) {
588 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
589 |     if (typeSet == null) {
590 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet = Sets.newHashSet());
591 |     }
592 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
593 |     typeSet.add(owner);
594 |   }

/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
401 |   @Override
402 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
403 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
404 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
405 |     if (unknown) {
406 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
407 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
408 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
409 |         unknown = false;
410 |       } else {
411 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
412 |       }
413 |     }
414 |     return unknown;
415 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
251 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
252 |     this.type = type;
253 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
254 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
255 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
256 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
257 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
258 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
259 |       int i = 0;
260 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
261 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
262 |         i++;
263 |       }
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
267 | 
268 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
269 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
270 |       return;
271 |     }
272 | 
273 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
274 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
275 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
276 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
277 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
278 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
279 |       trace.truncateEvents();
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
283 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
284 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
285 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
286 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
287 |                     + "try/finally block. "
288 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
289 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
290 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     trace.startEvent(this);
294 |   }

387 |           ALLOWED_DIRECTIVES.contains(n.getFirstChild().getString());

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 368-384
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
368 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
369 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
370 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
371 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
372 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
373 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
374 |         String inputName = input.getName();
375 | 
376 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
377 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
378 |         // will get an error down the line.
379 |         inputs.add(input);
380 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
381 |       }
382 |     }
383 |     return inputs;
384 |   }

Method code:
588 |   public void addChildToBack(Node child) {
589 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(child.parent == null);
590 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(child.next == null);
591 |     child.parent = this;
592 |     child.next = null;
593 |     if (last == null) {
594 |       first = last = child;
595 |       return;
596 |     }
597 |     last.next = child;
598 |     last = child;
599 |   }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 136-163
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
136 |   JSType build() {
137 |     if (result == null) {
138 |       if (isAllType) {
139 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
140 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
141 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
142 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
143 |         } else {
144 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
145 |         }
146 |       } else {
147 |         Set<JSType> alternateSet = ImmutableSet.copyOf(alternates);
148 |         int size = alternateSet.size();
149 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
150 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
151 |         } else {
152 |           if (size > 1) {
153 |             result = new UnionType(registry, alternateSet);
154 |           } else if (size == 1) {
155 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
156 |           } else {
157 |             result = registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
158 |           }
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 |     }
162 |     return result;
163 |   }

===== M0244 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSDocString(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 281-305
Documentation:

Method code:
281 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
282 |     switch (c) {
283 |       case '@':
284 |       case '*':
285 |       case ',':
286 |       case '>':
287 |       case ':':
288 |       case '(':
289 |       case ')':
290 |       case '{':
291 |       case '}':
292 |       case '[':
293 |       case ']':
294 |       case '?':
295 |       case '!':
296 |       case '|':
297 |       case '=':
298 |       case EOF_CHAR:
299 |       case '\n':
300 |         return false;
301 | 
302 |       default:
303 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
304 |     }
305 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processInfixExpression(InfixExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 575-590
Documentation:

Method code:
575 |     @Override
576 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
577 |       Node n =  new Node(
578 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
579 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
580 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
581 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
582 |       // doesn't do.
583 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
584 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned
585 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
586 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
587 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
588 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
589 |       return n;
590 |     }

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 151-227
Documentation:
/**
     *
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */
Method code:
151 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
152 |       int nType = n.getType();
153 |       switch (nType) {
154 |         case Token.NAME:
155 |         case Token.STRING:
156 |           String nString = n.getString();
157 |           if (nType == Token.NAME &&
158 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.CALL &&
159 |               "eval".equals(nString)) {
160 |             n.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
161 |           }
162 |           if (convention.isConstant(nString)) {
163 |             n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
164 |           }
165 |           break;
166 | 
167 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
168 |           JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
169 |           if (fnInfo == null) {
170 |             // Look for the info on other nodes.
171 |             if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
172 |               // on ASSIGNs
173 |               fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
174 |             } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
175 |               // on var NAME = function() { ... };
176 |               fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
177 |             }
178 |           }
179 | 
180 |           // Compute which function parameters are optional and
181 |           // which are var_args.
182 |           Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
183 |           for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
184 |                arg != null;
185 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
186 |             String argName = arg.getString();
187 |             JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
188 |                 null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
189 | 
190 |             if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
191 |                 typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
192 |               arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
193 |             }
194 |             if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
195 |                 typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
196 |               arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
197 |             }
198 |           }
199 |           break;
200 | 
201 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
202 |           if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
203 |             for (Node key = n.getFirstChild();
204 |                  key != null; key = key.getNext().getNext()) {
205 |               Node value = key.getNext();
206 |               if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
207 |                   key.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
208 |                 value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
209 |               }
210 |             }
211 |           }
212 |           break;
213 |       }
214 | 
215 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Determine if it is possible to simply use the javadoc
216 |       // everywhere rather than use IS_DISPATCHER.
217 |       /*
218 |        * Translate dispatcher info into the property expected node.
219 |        */
220 |       if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isJavaDispatch()) {
221 |         if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
222 |           Node fnNode = n.getLastChild();
223 |           Preconditions.checkState(fnNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
224 |           fnNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_DISPATCHER, true);
225 |         }
226 |       }
227 |     }

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param comment The JsDoc comment to parse.
   * @param lineno The line number of the node this comment is attached to.
   * @param fileLevelJsDocBuilder The builder for file-level JSDocInfo.

   *     parser may warn if another @fileoverview is found. May be null.

   *     normal jsdoc, or no jsdoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).

232 |       Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder,

237 |       new JsDocInfoParser(

245 |     jsdocParser.setFileOverviewJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
246 |     jsdocParser.parse();

===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 85-129
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
85 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
86 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
87 |     // just bail out early here.
88 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
89 |       return this;
90 |     }
91 | 
92 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
93 | 
94 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
95 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
96 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
97 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
98 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
99 |     }
100 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
101 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
102 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
103 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
104 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
105 |         }
106 |       } else {
107 |         if (!alternate.isUnknownType()) {
108 |           Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
109 |           while (it.hasNext()) {
110 |             JSType current = it.next();
111 |             if (!current.isUnknownType()) {
112 |               if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
113 |                 // Alternate is unnecessary.
114 |                 return this;
115 |               } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
116 |                 // Alternate makes current obsolete
117 |                 it.remove();
118 |               }
119 |             }
120 |           }
121 |         }
122 |         alternates.add(alternate);
123 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
124 |       }
125 |     } else {
126 |       result = null;
127 |     }
128 |     return this;
129 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args

106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;

Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1386 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1387 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1388 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1389 |       // top-level unions are allowed

1393 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1394 |           next();
1395 |         }
1396 |         skipEOLs();
1397 |         token = next();
1398 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1399 |       }
1400 |     }
1401 |     return typeExpr;
1402 |   }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1512-1534
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1512 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1513 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1514 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1515 |     }
1516 | 
1517 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1518 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1519 |       next();
1520 |       skipEOLs();
1521 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1522 |       if (memberType != null) {
1523 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1524 | 
1525 |         skipEOLs();
1526 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1527 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1528 |         }
1529 | 
1530 |         next();
1531 |       }
1532 |     }
1533 |     return typeName;
1534 |   }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1695-1709
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1695 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1696 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1697 |       return getString();
1698 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1699 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1700 |       if (left == null) {
1701 |         return null;
1702 |       }
1703 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1704 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1705 |       return "this";
1706 |     } else {
1707 |       return null;
1708 |     }
1709 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 364-379
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
364 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
365 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
366 |       Set<String> directives = null;
367 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
368 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
369 |         if (directives == null) {
370 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
371 |         } else {
372 |           directives.add(directive);
373 |         }
374 |       }
375 | 
376 |       if (directives != null) {
377 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
378 |       }
379 |     }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 297-305
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
297 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
298 |       Node ret = transform(n);
299 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
300 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
301 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
302 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
303 |       }
304 |       return ret;
305 |     }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 995-1122
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
995 |   Node parseInputs() {
996 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
997 | 
998 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
999 |     // individual file parse trees.
1000 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1001 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1002 |     }
1003 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1004 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1005 |     }
1006 | 
1007 |     // Parse main js sources.
1008 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1009 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1010 | 
1011 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1012 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1013 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1014 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1015 |     }
1016 | 
1017 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1018 | 
1019 |     try {
1020 |       // Parse externs sources.
1021 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1022 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1023 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1024 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1025 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1026 |           return null;
1027 |         }
1028 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1032 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1033 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1034 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1035 | 
1036 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1037 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1038 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1039 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1040 |           }
1041 |         }
1042 | 
1043 |         try {
1044 |           inputs =
1045 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1046 |               .manageDependencies(inputs);
1047 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1048 |           report(JSError.make(
1049 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1050 |           return null;
1051 |         }
1052 |       }
1053 | 
1054 |       // Check if externs files need to be lifted.
1055 |       boolean liftedExterns = false;
1056 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1057 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1058 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1059 |           return null;
1060 |         }
1061 | 
1062 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1063 |         if (n == null) {
1064 |           continue;
1065 |         }
1066 | 
1067 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isExterns()) {
1068 |           // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1069 |           // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1070 |           // the externs pile anyways.
1071 |           externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1072 |           input.setIsExtern(true);
1073 | 
1074 |           // TODO(nicksantos): We need a better mechanism to make sure
1075 |           // changes to the inputs array get reflected in JSModules.
1076 |           input.getModule().remove(input);
1077 | 
1078 |           externs.add(input);
1079 |           liftedExterns = true;
1080 |         }
1081 |       }
1082 | 
1083 |       if (liftedExterns) {
1084 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1085 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1086 |       }
1087 | 
1088 |       // Build the AST.
1089 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1090 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1091 |         if (n == null) {
1092 |           continue;
1093 |         }
1094 | 
1095 |         if (devMode) {
1096 |           runSanityCheck();
1097 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1098 |             return null;
1099 |           }
1100 |         }
1101 | 
1102 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1103 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1104 | 
1105 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1106 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1107 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1108 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(input.getName());
1109 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1110 |         }
1111 | 
1112 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1113 |       }
1114 | 
1115 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1116 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1117 | 
1118 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1119 |     } finally {
1120 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1121 |     }
1122 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;
450 |   }

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
311 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
312 |     if (c <= 127) {
313 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
314 |     } else {
315 |       return c == 0xA0
316 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
317 |     }
318 |   }

===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractBlockComment(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1206-1251
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1206 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1207 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1208 | 
1209 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1210 | 
1211 |     do {
1212 |       switch (token) {
1213 |         case ANNOTATION:
1214 |         case EOC:
1215 |         case EOF:
1216 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1217 | 
1218 |         case STAR:
1219 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1220 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1221 |               builder.append(' ');
1222 |             }
1223 | 
1224 |             builder.append('*');
1225 |           }
1226 | 
1227 |           token = next();
1228 |           continue;
1229 | 
1230 |         case EOL:
1231 |           ignoreStar = true;
1232 |           builder.append('\n');
1233 |           token = next();
1234 |           continue;
1235 | 
1236 |         default:
1237 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1238 |             builder.append(' ');
1239 |           }
1240 | 
1241 |           ignoreStar = false;
1242 | 
1243 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1244 | 
1245 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1246 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1247 |           builder.append(line);
1248 |           token = next();
1249 |       }
1250 |     } while (true);
1251 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
86 |       String sourceStr) {
87 |     try {
88 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
89 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
90 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
91 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
92 |           logger_);
93 |     } catch (IOException e) {
94 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
105 |     // the source file and module.
106 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
107 |   }

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 815-842
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
815 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
816 |     // unknown
817 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
818 |       return true;
819 |     }
820 |     // equality
821 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
822 |       return true;
823 |     }
824 |     // all type
825 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
826 |       return true;
827 |     }
828 |     // unions
829 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
830 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
831 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
832 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
833 |           return true;
834 |         }
835 |       }
836 |     }
837 |     // named types
838 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
839 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
840 |     }
841 |     return false;
842 |   }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckGlobalThis.getFunctionJsDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckGlobalThis.java
Original range: 167-183
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets a function's JSDoc information, if it has any. Checks for a few
   * patterns (ellipses show where JSDoc would be):
   * <pre>
   * ... function() {}
   * ... x = function() {};
   * var ... x = function() {};
   * ... var x = function() {};
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
167 |   private JSDocInfo getFunctionJsDocInfo(Node n) {
168 |     JSDocInfo jsDoc = n.getJSDocInfo();
169 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
170 |     if (jsDoc == null) {
171 |       int parentType = parent.getType();
172 |       if (parentType == Token.NAME || parentType == Token.ASSIGN) {
173 |         jsDoc = parent.getJSDocInfo();
174 |         if (jsDoc == null && parentType == Token.NAME) {
175 |           Node gramps = parent.getParent();
176 |           if (gramps.getType() == Token.VAR) {
177 |             jsDoc = gramps.getJSDocInfo();
178 |           }
179 |         }
180 |       }
181 |     }
182 |     return jsDoc;
183 |   }

   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1832 |   public Node cloneTree() {

===== M0373 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 184-214
Documentation:

Method code:
184 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
185 |     String jsDoc = node.getJsDoc();
186 |     NodeWithJsDoc nodeWithJsDoc = null;
187 |     if (jsDoc != null) {
188 |       nodeWithJsDoc = new NodeWithJsDoc();
189 |       nodesWithJsDoc.put(jsDoc, nodeWithJsDoc);
190 |     }
191 | 
192 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
193 |     if (nodeWithJsDoc != null) {
194 |       nodeWithJsDoc.node = irNode;
195 |     }
196 | 
197 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
198 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
199 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
200 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
201 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
204 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
205 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
206 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
207 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
208 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
209 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
210 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 |     return irNode;
214 |   }

===== M0381 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1115-1123
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1115 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1116 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1117 |       return -1;
1118 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1119 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1120 |     } else {
1121 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1122 |     }
1123 |   }

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 511-562
Documentation:

Method code:
511 |    @Override
512 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
513 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
514 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
515 |       if (name == null) {
516 |         name = new Name();
517 |         name.setIdentifier("");
518 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
519 |       }
520 |       Node node = new Node(Token.FUNCTION);
521 |       node.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, functionNode.getSourceName());
522 |       Node newName = transform(name);
523 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
524 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
525 |         // declaration.
526 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
527 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
528 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
529 |         // same line as the function keyword.
530 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
531 |             functionNode.getLp();
532 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
536 |       Node lp = new Node(Token.LP);
537 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
538 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
539 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
540 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
541 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
542 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
543 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
544 |       if (fnName != null) {
545 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
546 |       } else {
547 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
548 |       }
549 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
550 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
551 | 
552 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
553 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
554 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
555 |       }
556 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
557 | 
558 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
559 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
560 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
561 |      return node;
562 |     }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

===== M0396 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.IRFactory(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter)

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

/**
   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and
   * returns it. Once this method is called, the builder can be reused to build
   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param sourceName The source file containing the JSDoc.
   * @return a {@link JSDocInfo} object populated with the values given to this
   *     builder. If no value was populated, this method simply returns
   *     {@code null}
   */
Method code:
108 |   public JSDocInfo build(String sourceName) {
109 |     if (populated) {
110 |       JSDocInfo built = currentInfo;
111 |       built.setSourceName(sourceName);
112 |       populateDefaults(built);
113 |       populated = false;
114 |       currentInfo = new JSDocInfo(this.parseDocumentation);
115 |       return built;
116 |     } else {
117 |       return null;
118 |     }
119 |   }
